local ya=string local vx=math local ll=table local jd=bit32 local lm=ya.char local sl=ya.byte local uf=ya.sub local lb=ya.upper local jj=ya.lower local cu=ya.find local bj=ya.len local se=vx.floor local bg=vx.sqrt local sg=ll.insert local uj=ll.remove local ux=ll.concat local pk=jd.bxor local yi=jd.band local np=jd.bor local jb=jd.rshift local zl=jd.lshift local ps=jd.rrotate local ab=jd.bnot local od=jd.lrotate local go=rawset local fu=rawget local wm=tostring local j=tonumber local a=(function(uh,ui,uk,ul,um,un,uo,up,uq,ur,us)local b=[[!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!4!0;S#!Q$jNfhKkrX*De/@iM5T!GtsbOR[L?,mHU+A><q8IvcBE67a9=po!!!P^!VF_`ClY2':%"])Jd&ZWng3(-1\u!.!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!]]local function uv(c)local d=ur local function e(f)local g=d(b,d(c,f)+1)or 33 local h=g-33 return h>0 and h-1 or 0 end local i=e(1)+e(2)*85 local k=e(3)local l={}for m=4,#c-4,5 do local n=0 for o=0,4 do n=n*85+e(m+o)end l[#l+1]=ui(up(n,24),0xFF);l[#l+1]=ui(up(n,16),0xFF)l[#l+1]=ui(up(n,8),0xFF);l[#l+1]=ui(n,0xFF)end for p=1,k do l[#l]=nil end return l end local function ut(q,r,s)local t=q[r]or 0 return ui(uh(t,ui(s*131+r,0xFF)),0xFF)end local function uu(u,v)local w=uv(u)local x=w[1]local y=2 local z=ut(w,y,x);y=y+1 local aa=ui(uh(ut(w,y,x),z),0xFF);y=y+1 local ac={}for ad=1,aa do ac[ad]=uk(ui(uh(ut(w,y,x),z),0xFF));y=y+1 end local ae=ul(ac)local af={}for ad=1,6 do af[ad]=ui(uh(ut(w,y,x),z),0xFF);y=y+1 end local ag=af[3]local ah=af[1]local ai=af[6]local aj=af[2]local ak=af[5]local al=af[4]local am=ai+aj*256 local an={}for ad=1,ak do an[ad]=ui(uh(ut(w,y,x),z),0xFF);y=y+1 end local function ao()local ap=ag local aq={}for ad=1,ak do aq[ad]=uk(ui(uh(an[ad],ap),0xFF))ap=ui(ap*ah+ad,0xFF)end return ul(aq)end if al==1 then local ar=function(as)local at=0 if um(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+v)%65537~=am then return nil end return uq[ao()]end un(uq,ae,ar)elseif al==3 then local aw=function(as)local at=0 if um(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+v)%65537~=am then return nil end local ax=ao()local ay=loadstring(ax)if not ay then return nil end return ay()end un(uq,ae,aw)else local az=nil local fn=function(as)if az~=nil then return az end local at=0 if um(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+v)%65537~=am then return nil end az=ao()return az end un(uq,ae,fn)end local ba=v for ad=1,#ae do ba=(ui(ba*31,0xFFFF)+ur(ae,ad)+ad)%65537 end return ba end return function(bb)local bc=0 for ad=1,#bb do bc=uu(bb[ad],bc)end end
